  <section id="usb-device-stack">
<h1>USB Device Stack<a class="headerlink" href="#usb-device-stack" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h1>
<section id="overview">
<h2>Overview<a class="headerlink" href="#overview" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>The ESP-IDF USB Device Stack (hereinafter referred to as the Device Stack) enables USB Device support on ESP32-S3. By using the Device Stack, ESP32-S3 can be programmed with any well defined USB device functions (e.g., keyboard, mouse, camera), a custom function (aka vendor-specific class), or a combination of those functions (aka a composite device).</p>
<p>The Device Stack is built around the TinyUSB stack, but extends TinyUSB with some minor features and modifications for better integration with ESP-IDF. The Device stack is distributed as a managed component via the <a class="reference external" href="https://components.espressif.com/components/espressif/esp_tinyusb">ESP Component Registry</a>.</p>
</section>
<section id="features">
<h2>Features<a class="headerlink" href="#features" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h2>
<p><ul class="simple">
<li><dl class="simple">
<dt>1 USB Full-Speed (USB 1.1) peripheral with internal PHY</dt><dd><ul>
<li><p>Endpoint 0 and 6 additional endpoints configurable as IN or OUT</p></li>
<li><p>Up to 5 IN endpoints (including Endpoint 0) concurrently active.</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</li>
<li><p>Multiple supported device classes (CDC, HID, MIDI, MSC…)</p></li>
<li><p>Vendor specific class</p></li>
<li><p>Composite devices</p></li>
<li><p>VBUS monitoring for self-powered devices</p></li>
<li><p>Remote wakeup</p></li>
<li><p>Automatic light sleep control via ESP-IDF PM locks</p></li>
</ul>
</p>
</section>
<section id="hardware-connection">
<h2>Hardware Connection<a class="headerlink" href="#hardware-connection" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>The ESP32-S3 routes the <strong>USB 1.1 peripheral</strong> D+ and D- signals to GPIOs 20 and 19 respectively. For USB device functionality, these GPIOs must be connected to the bus (e.g., via a Micro-B port, USB-C port, or directly to standard-A plug).</p>
<figure class="align-center">
<img alt="Connection of an USB GPIOs directly to a USB standard-A plug" src="_images/usb-board-connection.png" />
</figure>
<div class="admonition note">
<p class="admonition-title">Note</p>
<p>If you are using an ESP32-S3 development board with two USB ports, the port labeled “USB” will already be connected to the D+ and D- GPIOs.</p>
</div>
<div class="admonition note">
<p class="admonition-title">Note</p>
<p>Self-powered devices must also connect VBUS through a voltage divider or comparator. For more details, please refer to <a class="reference internal" href="#self-powered-device"><span class="std std-ref">Self-Powered Device</span></a>.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section id="external-phy-configuration">
<h2>External PHY Configuration<a class="headerlink" href="#external-phy-configuration" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>The ESP32-S3 contains two USB controllers: USB-OTG and USB-Serial-JTAG. However, both controllers share a <strong>single PHY</strong>, which means only one can operate at a time. To use USB Device functionality while the USB-Serial-JTAG is active (e.g., for debugging or flashing), an <strong>external PHY</strong> is required, since the PHY is used by USB-Serial-JTAG.</p>
<div class="admonition note">
<p class="admonition-title">Note</p>
<p>An external PHY is not the only way to enable debugging alongside USB Host or Device functionality. It is also possible to switch the debugging interface from USB-Serial-JTAG to plain JTAG by burning the appropriate eFuses. For details, refer to document <a class="reference external" href="https://docs.espressif.com/projects/esp-idf/en/stable/esp32s3/api-guides/jtag-debugging/index.html">JTAG Debugging</a> in ESP-IDF Programming Guide for your target.</p>
</div>
<p>ESP32-S3 supports connecting external PHY ICs. This becomes especially relevant when full-speed USB device functionality is needed while the USB-Serial-JTAG controller is also in use. Various external PHY ICs may require different hardware modifications. Please refer to each IC’s datasheet for specifics. A general connection diagram below is provided for reference. For more information, please refer to <a class="reference external" href="https://docs.espressif.com/projects/esp-iot-solution/en/latest/usb/usb_overview/usb_phy.html#use-an-external-phy">Use an external PHY</a>.</p>
<figure class="align-center" id="id71">
<img alt="usb_fs_phy_sp5301" src="_images/usb_fs_phy_sp5301.png" />
<figcaption>
<p><span class="caption-text">A typical circuit diagram for an external PHY</span><a class="headerlink" href="#id71" title="Permalink to this image"></a></p>
</figcaption>
</figure>
<p><strong>List of Tested External PHY ICs:</strong></p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><strong>SP5301</strong> — Directly supported by ESP32-S3. See the guide above for schematic and routing information.</p></li>
<li><p><strong>TUSB1106</strong> — Directly supported by ESP32-S3. Works with the external-PHY driver via GPIO mapping. Follow the reference wiring in the TUSB1106 datasheet (power-supply options and recommended series resistors on D+/D–).</p></li>
<li><p><strong>STUSB03E</strong> — Requires signal routing using an analog switch. See example below.</p></li>
</ul>
<figure class="align-center" id="id72">
<img alt="External PHY with Analog Switch Schematic (Device mode)" src="_images/ext_phy_schematic_stusb03e.png" />
<figcaption>
<p><span class="caption-text">Example connection using STUSB03E and analog switch (Device mode)</span><a class="headerlink" href="#id72" title="Permalink to this image"></a></p>
</figcaption>
</figure>
<div class="admonition note">
<p class="admonition-title">Note</p>
<p>This schematic is a minimal example intended only to demonstrate the external PHY connection. It omits other essential components and signals (e.g., VCC, GND, RESET) required for a complete, functional ESP32-S3 design.
The schematic includes both a +5 V rail (usually from USB VBUS) and a VCC rail. VCC should match the chip supply voltage (usually 3.3 V). Ensure that the external PHY and the chip are powered from the same voltage domain. If designing a self-powered USB device, connect VBUSDET signal from the external PHY to ESP32-S3 for mandatory VBUS monitoring.</p>
</div>
<p>Hardware configuration is handled via GPIO mapping to the PHY’s pins. Any unused pins (e.g., <code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">usb_phy_ext_io_conf_t::suspend_n_io_num</span></code>, <code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">usb_phy_ext_io_conf_t::fs_edge_sel_io_num</span></code>) <strong>must be set to -1</strong>.</p>
<div class="admonition note">
<p class="admonition-title">Note</p>
<p>The <code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">usb_phy_ext_io_conf_t::suspend_n_io_num</span></code> pin is <strong>currently not supported</strong> and does not need to be connected.
The <code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">usb_phy_ext_io_conf_t::fs_edge_sel_io_num</span></code> pin is optional and only required if switching between low-speed and full-speed modes is needed.</p>
</div>
<p>Starting from version 2.0, the ESP TinyUSB Device Stack supports external PHY usage. To use an external PHY in device mode:</p>
<ol class="arabic simple">
<li><p>Configure the GPIO mapping and PHY using <code class="xref cpp cpp-type docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">usb_phy_config_t</span></code>.</p></li>
<li><p>Create the PHY using <code class="xref cpp cpp-func docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">usb_new_phy()</span></code>.</p></li>
<li><p>Use <code class="xref cpp cpp-func docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_DEFAULT_CONFIG()</span></code> to initialize <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv416tinyusb_config_t" title="tinyusb_config_t"><code class="xref cpp cpp-type docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_config_t</span></code></a>.</p></li>
<li><p>Set the <cite>phy.skip_setup</cite> field of <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv416tinyusb_config_t" title="tinyusb_config_t"><code class="xref cpp cpp-type docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_config_t</span></code></a> to <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">true</span></code> to bypass PHY reinitialization and use the externally configured PHY.</p></li>
</ol>
<p><strong>Example Code:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight-c notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span><span class="c1">// GPIO configuration for external PHY</span>
<span class="k">const</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">usb_phy_ext_io_conf_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">ext_io_conf</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="p">{</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">vp_io_num</span><span class="w">  </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="mi">8</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">vm_io_num</span><span class="w">  </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="mi">5</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">rcv_io_num</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="mi">11</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">oen_io_num</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="mi">17</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">vpo_io_num</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="mi">4</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">vmo_io_num</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="mi">46</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">suspend_n_io_num</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="mi">-1</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">fs_edge_sel_io_num</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="mi">-1</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="p">};</span>

<span class="c1">// Configuration and initialization of external PHY for OTG controller (Device mode)</span>
<span class="k">const</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">usb_phy_config_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">phy_config</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="p">{</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">controller</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">USB_PHY_CTRL_OTG</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">target</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">USB_PHY_TARGET_EXT</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">otg_mode</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">USB_OTG_MODE_DEVICE</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">otg_speed</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">USB_PHY_SPEED_FULL</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">ext_io_conf</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">&amp;</span><span class="n">ext_io_conf</span>
<span class="p">};</span>

<span class="n">usb_phy_handle_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">phy_hdl</span><span class="p">;</span>
<span class="n">ESP_ERROR_CHECK</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">usb_new_phy</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="o">&amp;</span><span class="n">phy_config</span><span class="p">,</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">&amp;</span><span class="n">phy_hdl</span><span class="p">));</span>

<span class="c1">// Initialize TinyUSB with default configuration (event handler can be set if needed)</span>
<span class="n">tinyusb_config_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">config</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">TINYUSB_DEFAULT_CONFIG</span><span class="p">();</span>
<span class="n">config</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">phy</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">skip_setup</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">true</span><span class="p">;</span>

<span class="n">tinyusb_driver_install</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="o">&amp;</span><span class="n">config</span><span class="p">);</span>
</pre></div>
</div>
<p>This setup ensures that the USB Device stack uses the <strong>external PHY</strong> instead of attempting to configure the internal one.</p>
</section>
<section id="device-stack-structure">
<h2>Device Stack Structure<a class="headerlink" href="#device-stack-structure" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>The basis of the Device Stack is TinyUSB, where the Device Stack implements the following features on top of TinyUSB:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p>Customization of USB descriptors</p></li>
<li><p>Serial device support</p></li>
<li><p>Redirecting of standard streams through the Serial device</p></li>
<li><p>Storage Media (SPI-Flash and SD-Card) for USB Device MSC Class.</p></li>
<li><p>A task within the encapsulated device stack that handles TinyUSB servicing</p></li>
</ul>
<section id="component-dependency">
<h3>Component Dependency<a class="headerlink" href="#component-dependency" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>
<p>The Device Stack is distributed via the <a class="reference external" href="https://components.espressif.com/components/espressif/esp_tinyusb">ESP Component Registry</a>. Thus, to use it, please add the Device Stack component as dependency using the following command:</p>
<div class="highlight-bash notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>idf.py<span class="w"> </span>add-dependency<span class="w"> </span>esp_tinyusb
</pre></div>
</div>
</section>
<section id="configuration-options">
<h3>Configuration Options<a class="headerlink" href="#configuration-options" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>
<p>Multiple aspects of the Device Stack can be configured using menuconfig. These include:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p>The verbosity of the TinyUSB’s log</p></li>
<li><p>Device Stack task related options</p></li>
<li><p>Default device/string descriptor options</p></li>
<li><p>Class specific options</p></li>
<li><p>Power management options (PM locks and USB wakeup)</p></li>
</ul>
<p>The generated Kconfig reference for the available <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">CONFIG_TINYUSB_*</span></code> options is provided at the end of this document.</p>
</section>
<section id="descriptor-configuration">
<span id="descriptors-configuration"></span><h3>Descriptor Configuration<a class="headerlink" href="#descriptor-configuration" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>
<p>The <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv416tinyusb_config_t" title="tinyusb_config_t"><code class="xref cpp cpp-type docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_config_t</span></code></a> structure exposes USB descriptor settings through <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N16tinyusb_config_t10descriptorE" title="tinyusb_config_t::descriptor"><code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_config_t::descriptor</span></code></a>, which is a <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv421tinyusb_desc_config_t" title="tinyusb_desc_config_t"><code class="xref cpp cpp-type docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_desc_config_t</span></code></a>.</p>
<p>The following descriptors should be initialized for both full-speed and high-speed devices:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N21tinyusb_desc_config_t6deviceE" title="tinyusb_desc_config_t::device"><code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_desc_config_t::device</span></code></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N21tinyusb_desc_config_t6stringE" title="tinyusb_desc_config_t::string"><code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_desc_config_t::string</span></code></a></p></li>
</ul>
<p>Full-speed devices should initialize the following field to provide their configuration descriptor:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N21tinyusb_desc_config_t17full_speed_configE" title="tinyusb_desc_config_t::full_speed_config"><code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_desc_config_t::full_speed_config</span></code></a></p></li>
</ul>
<p>The Device Stack will instantiate a USB device based on the descriptors provided in the fields described above when <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv422tinyusb_driver_installPK16tinyusb_config_t" title="tinyusb_driver_install"><code class="xref cpp cpp-func docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_driver_install()</span></code></a> is called.</p>
<p>The Device Stack also provides default descriptors when the corresponding fields in <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv421tinyusb_desc_config_t" title="tinyusb_desc_config_t"><code class="xref cpp cpp-type docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_desc_config_t</span></code></a> are set to <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">NULL</span></code>. Their values come from menuconfig options such as <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">CONFIG_TINYUSB_DESC_*</span></code>. Default descriptors include:</p>
<ul>
<li><p>Default device descriptor: set <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N21tinyusb_desc_config_t6deviceE" title="tinyusb_desc_config_t::device"><code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_desc_config_t::device</span></code></a> to <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">NULL</span></code>.</p></li>
<li><p>Default string descriptor: set <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N21tinyusb_desc_config_t6stringE" title="tinyusb_desc_config_t::string"><code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_desc_config_t::string</span></code></a> to <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">NULL</span></code>.</p></li>
<li><p>Default qualifier descriptor: set <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N21tinyusb_desc_config_t9qualifierE" title="tinyusb_desc_config_t::qualifier"><code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_desc_config_t::qualifier</span></code></a> to <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">NULL</span></code> on high-speed-capable devices.</p></li>
<li><p>Default configuration descriptors. Some classes that rarely require custom configuration (such as CDC, MSC, and NCM) provide default configuration descriptors. These can be enabled by setting the associated field to <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">NULL</span></code>:</p>
<blockquote>
<div><ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N21tinyusb_desc_config_t17full_speed_configE" title="tinyusb_desc_config_t::full_speed_config"><code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_desc_config_t::full_speed_config</span></code></a>: full-speed descriptor</p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N21tinyusb_desc_config_t17high_speed_configE" title="tinyusb_desc_config_t::high_speed_config"><code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_desc_config_t::high_speed_config</span></code></a>: high-speed descriptor on high-speed-capable devices</p></li>
</ul>
</div></blockquote>
</li>
</ul>
</section>
</section>
<section id="installation">
<h2>Installation<a class="headerlink" href="#installation" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>To install the Device Stack, please call <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv422tinyusb_driver_installPK16tinyusb_config_t" title="tinyusb_driver_install"><code class="xref cpp cpp-func docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_driver_install()</span></code></a>. The Device Stack’s configuration is specified in a <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv416tinyusb_config_t" title="tinyusb_config_t"><code class="xref cpp cpp-type docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_config_t</span></code></a> structure that is passed as an argument to <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv422tinyusb_driver_installPK16tinyusb_config_t" title="tinyusb_driver_install"><code class="xref cpp cpp-func docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_driver_install()</span></code></a>.</p>
<div class="admonition note">
<p class="admonition-title">Note</p>
<p>Initialize <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv416tinyusb_config_t" title="tinyusb_config_t"><code class="xref cpp cpp-type docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_config_t</span></code></a> with <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_DEFAULT_CONFIG()</span></code> and then override only the fields you need. Descriptor pointers left as <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">NULL</span></code> use the default descriptor values when available.</p>
</div>
<div class="highlight-c notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span><span class="cp">#include</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="cpf">"tinyusb_default_config.h"</span>

<span class="n">tinyusb_config_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">tusb_cfg</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">TINYUSB_DEFAULT_CONFIG</span><span class="p">();</span>
<span class="n">tusb_cfg</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">descriptor</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">device</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">NULL</span><span class="p">;</span>
<span class="n">tusb_cfg</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">descriptor</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">string</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">NULL</span><span class="p">;</span>
<span class="n">tusb_cfg</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">descriptor</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">full_speed_config</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">NULL</span><span class="p">;</span>
<span class="cp">#if (SOC_USB_OTG_PERIPH_NUM &gt; 1)</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="n">tusb_cfg</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">descriptor</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">high_speed_config</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">NULL</span><span class="p">;</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="n">tusb_cfg</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">descriptor</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">qualifier</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">NULL</span><span class="p">;</span>
<span class="cp">#endif</span>

<span class="n">ESP_ERROR_CHECK</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">tinyusb_driver_install</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="o">&amp;</span><span class="n">tusb_cfg</span><span class="p">));</span>
</pre></div>
</div>
</section>
<section id="self-powered-device">
<span id="id1"></span><h2>Self-Powered Device<a class="headerlink" href="#self-powered-device" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>USB specification mandates self-powered devices to monitor voltage levels on USB’s VBUS signal. As opposed to bus-powered devices, a self-powered device can be fully functional even without a USB connection. The self-powered device detects connection and disconnection events by monitoring the VBUS voltage level. VBUS is considered valid if it rises above 4.75 V and invalid if it falls below 4.35 V.</p>
<p>On the ESP32-S3, this will require using a GPIO to act as a voltage sensing pin to detect when VBUS goes above/below the prescribed thresholds. However, ESP32-S3 pins are 3.3 V tolerant. Thus, even if VBUS rises/falls above/below the thresholds mentioned above, it would still appear as a logic HIGH to the ESP32-S3. Thus, in order to detect the VBUS valid condition, users can do one of the following:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p>Connect VBUS to a voltage comparator chip/circuit that detects the thresholds described above (i.e., 4.35 V and 4.75 V), and outputs a 3.3 V logic level to the ESP32-S3 indicating whether VBUS is valid or not.</p></li>
<li><p>Use a resistor voltage divider that outputs (0.75 x Vdd) if VBUS is 4.4 V (see figure below).</p></li>
</ul>
<div class="admonition note">
<p class="admonition-title">Note</p>
<p>In either case, the voltage on the sensing pin must be logic low within 3 ms after the device is unplugged from the USB host.</p>
</div>
<figure class="align-center" id="id73">
<img alt="Simple voltage divider for VBUS monitoring" src="_images/usb_vbus_voltage_monitor.png" />
<figcaption>
<p><span class="caption-text">Simple voltage divider for VBUS monitoring</span><a class="headerlink" href="#id73" title="Permalink to this image"></a></p>
</figcaption>
</figure>
<p>To use this feature, set <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N20tinyusb_phy_config_t12self_poweredE" title="tinyusb_phy_config_t::self_powered"><code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_phy_config_t::self_powered</span></code></a> to <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">true</span></code> and <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N20tinyusb_phy_config_t15vbus_monitor_ioE" title="tinyusb_phy_config_t::vbus_monitor_io"><code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_phy_config_t::vbus_monitor_io</span></code></a> to the GPIO used for VBUS monitoring through <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N16tinyusb_config_t3phyE" title="tinyusb_config_t::phy"><code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_config_t::phy</span></code></a>.</p>
</section>
<section id="power-management">
<h2>Power Management<a class="headerlink" href="#power-management" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h2>
<section id="suspend-and-resume-events">
<h3>Suspend and Resume Events<a class="headerlink" href="#suspend-and-resume-events" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>
<p>Suspend and resume device events are optional and disabled by default. Enable the following Kconfig options to receive them through the USB Device Event callback (<a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N16tinyusb_config_t8event_cbE" title="tinyusb_config_t::event_cb"><code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_config_t::event_cb</span></code></a>):</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-suspend-callback"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_SUSPEND_CALLBACK</span></a> enables <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N18tinyusb_event_id_t23TINYUSB_EVENT_SUSPENDEDE" title="tinyusb_event_id_t::TINYUSB_EVENT_SUSPENDED"><code class="xref cpp cpp-enumerator docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_EVENT_SUSPENDED</span></code></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-resume-callback"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_RESUME_CALLBACK</span></a> enables <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N18tinyusb_event_id_t21TINYUSB_EVENT_RESUMEDE" title="tinyusb_event_id_t::TINYUSB_EVENT_RESUMED"><code class="xref cpp cpp-enumerator docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_EVENT_RESUMED</span></code></a></p></li>
</ul>
<p>When these options are enabled, esp_tinyusb provides strong implementations of <code class="xref cpp cpp-func docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tud_suspend_cb()</span></code> and <code class="xref cpp cpp-func docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tud_resume_cb()</span></code>. User applications <strong>must not</strong> define these callbacks themselves, or a linker error will occur due to multiple definitions.</p>
<p>If the Kconfig options are disabled, users may implement <code class="xref cpp cpp-func docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tud_suspend_cb()</span></code> and <code class="xref cpp cpp-func docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tud_resume_cb()</span></code> directly in the application instead.</p>
</section>
<section id="remote-wakeup">
<h3>Remote Wakeup<a class="headerlink" href="#remote-wakeup" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>
<p>When the host suspends the device and enables remote wakeup, the device can signal resume with <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv421tinyusb_remote_wakeupv" title="tinyusb_remote_wakeup"><code class="xref cpp cpp-func docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_remote_wakeup()</span></code></a>. Call this function only while the device is suspended and the host has enabled remote wakeup for the current suspend cycle.</p>
</section>
<section id="automatic-light-sleep-via-pm-locks">
<h3>Automatic Light Sleep via PM Locks<a class="headerlink" href="#automatic-light-sleep-via-pm-locks" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>
<p>esp_tinyusb can manage an <a class="reference external" href="https://docs.espressif.com/projects/esp-idf/en/latest/esp32s3/api-reference/system/power_management.html#_CPPv4N18esp_pm_lock_type_t21ESP_PM_NO_LIGHT_SLEEPE" title="(in ESP-IDF Programming Guide vmaster)"><code class="xref cpp cpp-enumerator docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">esp_pm_lock_type_t::ESP_PM_NO_LIGHT_SLEEP</span></code></a> lock based on USB device state. While the lock is held, the SoC will not enter automatic light sleep. When the USB host suspends the bus, esp_tinyusb releases the lock so automatic light sleep can proceed.</p>
<p>Enable this feature in two steps, using Kconfig options and runtime settings:</p>
<section id="kconfig-prerequisites">
<h4>Kconfig prerequisites<a class="headerlink" href="#kconfig-prerequisites" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<blockquote>
<div><p>Enable <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-pm"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_PM</span></a> in menuconfig. This option requires the following config options:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-suspend-callback"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_SUSPEND_CALLBACK</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-resume-callback"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_RESUME_CALLBACK</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.espressif.com/projects/esp-idf/en/latest/esp32s3/api-reference/kconfig-reference.html#config-pm-enable" title="(in ESP-IDF Programming Guide vmaster)"><span class="xref std std-ref">CONFIG_PM_ENABLE</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.espressif.com/projects/esp-idf/en/latest/esp32s3/api-reference/kconfig-reference.html#config-freertos-use-tickless-idle" title="(in ESP-IDF Programming Guide vmaster)"><span class="xref std std-ref">CONFIG_FREERTOS_USE_TICKLESS_IDLE</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
<section id="runtime-settings">
<h4>Runtime settings<a class="headerlink" href="#runtime-settings" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<blockquote>
<div><ul class="simple">
<li><p>configure the ESP-IDF PM module with the <a class="reference external" href="https://docs.espressif.com/projects/esp-idf/en/latest/esp32s3/api-reference/system/power_management.html#_CPPv4N15esp_pm_config_t18light_sleep_enableE" title="(in ESP-IDF Programming Guide vmaster)"><code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">esp_pm_config_t::light_sleep_enable</span></code></a> set to <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">true</span></code>,</p></li>
<li><p>set <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N16tinyusb_config_t14pm_lock_enableE" title="tinyusb_config_t::pm_lock_enable"><code class="xref cpp cpp-member docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_config_t::pm_lock_enable</span></code></a> to <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">true</span></code> when calling <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv422tinyusb_driver_installPK16tinyusb_config_t" title="tinyusb_driver_install"><code class="xref cpp cpp-func docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_driver_install()</span></code></a>.</p></li>
</ul>
</div></blockquote>
<p>Configure automatic light sleep through ESP-IDF PM:</p>
<div class="highlight-c notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span><span class="n">esp_pm_config_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">pm_config</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="p">{</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">max_freq_mhz</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">CONFIG_ESP_DEFAULT_CPU_FREQ_MHZ</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">min_freq_mhz</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">CONFIG_ESP_DEFAULT_CPU_FREQ_MHZ</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">light_sleep_enable</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">true</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="p">};</span>
<span class="n">ESP_ERROR_CHECK</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">esp_pm_configure</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="o">&amp;</span><span class="n">pm_config</span><span class="p">));</span>

<span class="n">tinyusb_config_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">tusb_cfg</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">TINYUSB_DEFAULT_CONFIG</span><span class="p">();</span>
<span class="n">tusb_cfg</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">pm_lock_enable</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">true</span><span class="p">;</span>
<span class="n">ESP_ERROR_CHECK</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">tinyusb_driver_install</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="o">&amp;</span><span class="n">tusb_cfg</span><span class="p">));</span>
</pre></div>
</div>
</section>
<section id="lock-behavior">
<h4>Lock behavior<a class="headerlink" href="#lock-behavior" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p>On driver install, esp_tinyusb creates an <a class="reference external" href="https://docs.espressif.com/projects/esp-idf/en/latest/esp32s3/api-reference/system/power_management.html#_CPPv4N18esp_pm_lock_type_t21ESP_PM_NO_LIGHT_SLEEPE" title="(in ESP-IDF Programming Guide vmaster)"><code class="xref cpp cpp-enumerator docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">esp_pm_lock_type_t::ESP_PM_NO_LIGHT_SLEEP</span></code></a> lock and acquires it.</p></li>
<li><p>On <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N18tinyusb_event_id_t22TINYUSB_EVENT_ATTACHEDE" title="tinyusb_event_id_t::TINYUSB_EVENT_ATTACHED"><code class="xref cpp cpp-enumerator docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_EVENT_ATTACHED</span></code></a>, the lock is acquired if it is not already held.</p></li>
<li><p>On <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N18tinyusb_event_id_t23TINYUSB_EVENT_SUSPENDEDE" title="tinyusb_event_id_t::TINYUSB_EVENT_SUSPENDED"><code class="xref cpp cpp-enumerator docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_EVENT_SUSPENDED</span></code></a>, the lock is released while the device remains mounted, allowing automatic light sleep.</p></li>
<li><p>On <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N18tinyusb_event_id_t21TINYUSB_EVENT_RESUMEDE" title="tinyusb_event_id_t::TINYUSB_EVENT_RESUMED"><code class="xref cpp cpp-enumerator docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_EVENT_RESUMED</span></code></a>, the lock is re-acquired.</p></li>
<li><p>On <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv4N18tinyusb_event_id_t22TINYUSB_EVENT_DETACHEDE" title="tinyusb_event_id_t::TINYUSB_EVENT_DETACHED"><code class="xref cpp cpp-enumerator docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_EVENT_DETACHED</span></code></a>, the lock is acquired and the suspend PM state is cleared.</p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv421tinyusb_remote_wakeupv" title="tinyusb_remote_wakeup"><code class="xref cpp cpp-func docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_remote_wakeup()</span></code></a> acquires the lock before signaling remote wakeup to the host.</p></li>
</ul>
<p>Suspend and Resume callbacks <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-suspend-callback"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_SUSPEND_CALLBACK</span></a> and <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-resume-callback"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_RESUME_CALLBACK</span></a> must be enabled, registered inside the esp_tinyusb, otherwise the PM module would not have information about the device being suspended or resumed.</p>
<p>Use <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv426tinyusb_pm_get_lock_statusPb" title="tinyusb_pm_get_lock_status"><code class="xref cpp cpp-func docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_pm_get_lock_status()</span></code></a> to query whether the PM lock is currently held.</p>
</section>
</section>
<section id="light-sleep-with-usb-wakeup">
<h3>Light Sleep with USB Wakeup<a class="headerlink" href="#light-sleep-with-usb-wakeup" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>
<p>On targets with full-speed port, the USB device peripheral can <strong>NOT</strong> be used as a light sleep wake-up source due to USB PHY limitations. This feature is only supported on high-speed ports.</p>
</section>
</section>
<section id="usb-serial-device-cdc-acm">
<h2>USB Serial Device (CDC-ACM)<a class="headerlink" href="#usb-serial-device-cdc-acm" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>If <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-cdc-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_CDC_ENABLED</span></a> is enabled in menuconfig, the USB Serial Device can be initialized with <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv419tinyusb_cdcacm_initPK23tinyusb_config_cdcacm_t" title="tinyusb_cdcacm_init"><code class="xref cpp cpp-func docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_cdcacm_init()</span></code></a> according to the settings from <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv423tinyusb_config_cdcacm_t" title="tinyusb_config_cdcacm_t"><code class="xref cpp cpp-type docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_config_cdcacm_t</span></code></a>, see the example below.</p>
<div class="highlight-c notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span><span class="k">const</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">tinyusb_config_cdcacm_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">acm_cfg</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="p">{</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">cdc_port</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">TINYUSB_CDC_ACM_0</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">callback_rx</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">NULL</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">callback_rx_wanted_char</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">NULL</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">callback_line_state_changed</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">NULL</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">callback_line_coding_changed</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">NULL</span>
<span class="p">};</span>
<span class="n">ESP_ERROR_CHECK</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">tinyusb_cdcacm_init</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="o">&amp;</span><span class="n">acm_cfg</span><span class="p">));</span>
</pre></div>
</div>
<p>To specify callbacks, you can either set the pointer to your <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv422tusb_cdcacm_callback_t" title="tusb_cdcacm_callback_t"><code class="xref cpp cpp-type docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tusb_cdcacm_callback_t</span></code></a> function in the configuration structure or call <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv432tinyusb_cdcacm_register_callback20tinyusb_cdcacm_itf_t19cdcacm_event_type_t22tusb_cdcacm_callback_t" title="tinyusb_cdcacm_register_callback"><code class="xref cpp cpp-func docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_cdcacm_register_callback()</span></code></a> after initialization.</p>
<section id="usb-serial-console">
<h3>USB Serial Console<a class="headerlink" href="#usb-serial-console" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>
<p>The USB Serial Device allows the redirection of all standard input/output streams (stdin, stdout, stderr) to USB. Thus, calling standard library input/output functions such as <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">printf()</span></code> will result into the data being sent/received over USB instead of UART.</p>
<p>Users should call <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv420tinyusb_console_initi" title="tinyusb_console_init"><code class="xref cpp cpp-func docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_console_init()</span></code></a> with the CDC interface number to switch the standard input/output streams to USB, and <a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv422tinyusb_console_deiniti" title="tinyusb_console_deinit"><code class="xref cpp cpp-func docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">tinyusb_console_deinit()</span></code></a> to switch them back to UART.</p>
</section>
</section>
<section id="usb-mass-storage-device-msc">
<h2>USB Mass Storage Device (MSC)<a class="headerlink" href="#usb-mass-storage-device-msc" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>If <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-msc-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MSC_ENABLED</span></a> is enabled in menuconfig, the ESP chip can be used as a USB MSC device. The storage media (SPI flash or SD card) can be initialized as shown below.</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p>SPI-Flash</p></li>
</ul>
<div class="highlight-c notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span><span class="k">static</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">esp_err_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">storage_init_spiflash</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">wl_handle_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">*</span><span class="n">wl_handle</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="p">{</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="o">***</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="n">esp_partition_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">*</span><span class="n">data_partition</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">esp_partition_find_first</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">ESP_PARTITION_TYPE_DATA</span><span class="p">,</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">ESP_PARTITION_SUBTYPE_DATA_FAT</span><span class="p">,</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">NULL</span><span class="p">);</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="o">***</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="n">wl_mount</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">data_partition</span><span class="p">,</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">wl_handle</span><span class="p">);</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="o">***</span>
<span class="p">}</span>
<span class="n">storage_init_spiflash</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="o">&amp;</span><span class="n">wl_handle</span><span class="p">);</span>

<span class="n">tinyusb_msc_storage_handle_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">storage_hdl</span><span class="p">;</span>
<span class="k">const</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">tinyusb_msc_storage_config_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">config_spi</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="p">{</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">medium</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">wl_handle</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">wl_handle</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="p">};</span>
<span class="n">ESP_ERROR_CHECK</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">tinyusb_msc_new_storage_spiflash</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="o">&amp;</span><span class="n">config_spi</span><span class="p">,</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">&amp;</span><span class="n">storage_hdl</span><span class="p">));</span>
</pre></div>
</div>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p>SD-Card</p></li>
</ul>
<div class="highlight-c notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span><span class="k">static</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">esp_err_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nf">storage_init_sdmmc</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">sdmmc_card_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">**</span><span class="n">card</span><span class="p">)</span>
<span class="p">{</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="o">***</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="n">sdmmc_host_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">host</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">SDMMC_HOST_DEFAULT</span><span class="p">();</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="n">sdmmc_slot_config_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">slot_config</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">SDMMC_SLOT_CONFIG_DEFAULT</span><span class="p">();</span>

<span class="w">    </span><span class="n">slot_config</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">cmd</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">CONFIG_EXAMPLE_PIN_CMD</span><span class="p">;</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="n">slot_config</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">d0</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">CONFIG_EXAMPLE_PIN_D0</span><span class="p">;</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="n">slot_config</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">d1</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">CONFIG_EXAMPLE_PIN_D1</span><span class="p">;</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="n">slot_config</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">d2</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">CONFIG_EXAMPLE_PIN_D2</span><span class="p">;</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="n">slot_config</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">d3</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">CONFIG_EXAMPLE_PIN_D3</span><span class="p">;</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="n">slot_config</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">flags</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">|=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">SDMMC_SLOT_FLAG_INTERNAL_PULLUP</span><span class="p">;</span>

<span class="w">    </span><span class="o">*</span><span class="n">card</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">calloc</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="mi">1</span><span class="p">,</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="k">sizeof</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">sdmmc_card_t</span><span class="p">));</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="n">ESP_ERROR_CHECK</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">sdmmc_host_init</span><span class="p">());</span>

<span class="w">    </span><span class="n">ESP_ERROR_CHECK</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">sdmmc_card_init</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="o">&amp;</span><span class="n">host</span><span class="p">,</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">*</span><span class="n">card</span><span class="p">));</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="o">***</span>
<span class="p">}</span>
<span class="n">sdmmc_card_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">*</span><span class="n">card</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nb">NULL</span><span class="p">;</span>
<span class="n">storage_init_sdmmc</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="o">&amp;</span><span class="n">card</span><span class="p">);</span>

<span class="n">tinyusb_msc_storage_handle_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">storage_hdl</span><span class="p">;</span>
<span class="k">const</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">tinyusb_msc_storage_config_t</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">config_sdmmc</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="p">{</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">medium</span><span class="p">.</span><span class="n">card</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">=</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n">card</span><span class="p">,</span>
<span class="p">};</span>
<span class="n">ESP_ERROR_CHECK</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="n">tinyusb_msc_new_storage_sdmmc</span><span class="p">(</span><span class="o">&amp;</span><span class="n">config_sdmmc</span><span class="p">,</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="o">&amp;</span><span class="n">storage_hdl</span><span class="p">));</span>
</pre></div>
</div>
<section id="msc-performance-optimization">
<h3>MSC Performance Optimization<a class="headerlink" href="#msc-performance-optimization" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>
<p><strong>Single-Buffer Approach</strong></p>
<p>The single-buffer approach improves performance by using a dedicated buffer to temporarily store incoming write data instead of processing it immediately in the callback.</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><strong>Configurable buffer size</strong>: The buffer size is set via <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-msc-bufsize"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MSC_BUFSIZE</span></a>, allowing users to balance performance and memory usage.</p></li>
</ul>
<p>This approach ensures that USB transactions remain fast while avoiding potential delays caused by storage operations.</p>
<p><strong>USB MSC Drive Performance</strong></p>
<table class="docutils align-default">
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 33.3%" />
<col style="width: 33.3%" />
<col style="width: 33.3%" />
</colgroup>
<thead>
<tr class="row-odd"><th class="head"><p>FIFO Size</p></th>
<th class="head"><p>Read Speed</p></th>
<th class="head"><p>Write Speed</p></th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr class="row-even"><td><p>512 B</p></td>
<td><p>0.566 MB/s</p></td>
<td><p>0.236 MB/s</p></td>
</tr>
<tr class="row-odd"><td><p>8192 B</p></td>
<td><p>0.925 MB/s</p></td>
<td><p>0.928 MB/s</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>Performance Limitations:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><strong>Internal SPI Flash performance</strong> is constrained by architectural limitations where program execution and storage access share the same flash chip. This results in program execution being <strong>suspended during flash writes</strong>, significantly impacting performance.</p></li>
<li><p><strong>Internal SPI Flash usage is intended primarily for demonstration purposes.</strong> For practical use cases requiring higher performance, it is recommended to use <strong>external storage such as an SD card or an external SPI flash chip, where supported.</strong></p></li>
</ul>
<p>SD cards are not affected by this constraint, explaining their higher performance gains.</p>
</section>
</section>
<section id="application-examples">
<h2>Application Examples<a class="headerlink" href="#application-examples" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>For better visibility, the examples can be found in ESP-IDF’s GitHub repository in the directory <a class="reference external" href="https://github.com/espressif/esp-idf/tree/master/examples/peripherals/usb/device">peripherals/usb/device</a>.</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://github.com/espressif/esp-idf/tree/master/examples/peripherals/usb/device/tusb_console">tusb_console</a> demonstrates how to set up ESP32-S3 to get log output via a Serial Device connection using the TinyUSB component, applicable for any Espressif boards that support USB-OTG.</p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://github.com/espressif/esp-idf/tree/master/examples/peripherals/usb/device/tusb_serial_device">tusb_serial_device</a> demonstrates how to set up ESP32-S3 to function as a USB Serial Device using the TinyUSB component, with the ability to be configured as a double serial device.</p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://github.com/espressif/esp-idf/tree/master/examples/peripherals/usb/device/tusb_midi">tusb_midi</a> demonstrates how to set up ESP32-S3 to function as a USB MIDI Device, outputting a MIDI note sequence via the native USB port using the TinyUSB component.</p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://github.com/espressif/esp-idf/tree/master/examples/peripherals/usb/device/tusb_hid">tusb_hid</a> demonstrates how to implement a USB keyboard and mouse using the TinyUSB component, which sends ‘key a/A pressed &amp; released’ events and moves the mouse in a square trajectory upon connection to a USB host.</p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://github.com/espressif/esp-idf/tree/master/examples/peripherals/usb/device/tusb_msc">tusb_msc</a> demonstrates how to use the USB capabilities to create a Mass Storage Device that can be recognized by USB-hosts, allowing access to its internal data storage, with support for SPI Flash and SD MMC Card storage media.</p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://github.com/espressif/esp-idf/tree/master/examples/peripherals/usb/device/tusb_composite_msc_serialdevice">tusb_composite_msc_serialdevice</a> demonstrates how to set up ESP32-S3 to function simultaneously as both a USB Serial Device and an MSC device (SPI-Flash as the storage media) using the TinyUSB component.</p></li>
</ul>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://github.com/espressif/esp-idf/tree/master/examples/peripherals/usb/device/tusb_ncm">tusb_ncm</a> demonstrates how to transmit Wi-Fi data to a Linux or Windows host via USB using the Network Control Model (NCM), a sub-class of Communication Device Class (CDC) USB Device for Ethernet-over-USB applications, with the help of a TinyUSB component.</p></li>
</ul>
</section>

<section id="functions">
<h3>Functions<a class="headerlink" href="#functions" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>
<dl class="cpp function">
<dt class="sig sig-object cpp" id="_CPPv422tinyusb_driver_installPK16tinyusb_config_t">
<span id="_CPPv322tinyusb_driver_installPK16tinyusb_config_t"></span><span id="_CPPv222tinyusb_driver_installPK16tinyusb_config_t"></span><span id="tinyusb_driver_install__tinyusb_config_tCP"></span><span class="target" id="tinyusb_8h_1a71b7fa1b1d97afa9bda62b9c2eff370e"></span><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.espressif.com/projects/esp-idf/en/latest/esp32s3/api-reference/system/esp_err.html#_CPPv49esp_err_t" title="(in ESP-IDF Programming Guide vmaster)"><span class="n"><span class="pre">esp_err_t</span></span></a><span class="w"> </span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="n"><span class="pre">tinyusb_driver_install</span></span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><span class="k"><span class="pre">const</span></span><span class="w"> </span><a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv416tinyusb_config_t" title="tinyusb_config_t"><span class="n"><span class="pre">tinyusb_config_t</span></span></a><span class="w"> </span><span class="p"><span class="pre">*</span></span><span class="n sig-param"><span class="pre">config</span></span><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="headerlink" href="#_CPPv422tinyusb_driver_installPK16tinyusb_config_t" title="Permalink to this definition"></a><br /></dt>
<dd><p>Install the TinyUSB device driver and start the TinyUSB task. </p>
<p>This helper configures the USB PHY when requested, prepares descriptors, initializes the TinyUSB stack, and starts the TinyUSB task.</p>
<div class="admonition note">
<p class="admonition-title">Note</p>
<p>When supplying a custom composite device descriptor with an Interface Association Descriptor, keep <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">bDeviceClass</span></code> as <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">TUSB_CLASS_MISC</span></code> and <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">bDeviceSubClass</span></code> as <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">MISC_SUBCLASS_COMMON</span></code>.</p>
</div>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Parameters<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p><strong>config</strong> – <strong>[in]</strong> TinyUSB stack configuration.</p>
</dd>
<dt class="field-even">Returns<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-even"><p><ul class="simple">
<li><p>ESP_OK on success</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG if <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">config</span></code> is NULL or contains unsupported port or task settings</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE if the TinyUSB device task is already running</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_NO_MEM if memory allocation fails during startup</p></li>
<li><p>Other error codes from TinyUSB task startup, USB PHY setup, descriptor setup, or power management initialization </p></li>
</ul>
</p>
</dd>
</dl>
</dd></dl>

<dl class="cpp function">
<dt class="sig sig-object cpp" id="_CPPv424tinyusb_driver_uninstallv">
<span id="_CPPv324tinyusb_driver_uninstallv"></span><span id="_CPPv224tinyusb_driver_uninstallv"></span><span id="tinyusb_driver_uninstall__void"></span><span class="target" id="tinyusb_8h_1a6a82bb8d27eaab5fbaac6d9f547616b2"></span><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.espressif.com/projects/esp-idf/en/latest/esp32s3/api-reference/system/esp_err.html#_CPPv49esp_err_t" title="(in ESP-IDF Programming Guide vmaster)"><span class="n"><span class="pre">esp_err_t</span></span></a><span class="w"> </span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="n"><span class="pre">tinyusb_driver_uninstall</span></span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><span class="kt"><span class="pre">void</span></span><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="headerlink" href="#_CPPv424tinyusb_driver_uninstallv" title="Permalink to this definition"></a><br /></dt>
<dd><p>Uninstall the TinyUSB device driver. </p>
<p>This stops the TinyUSB task, tears down the TinyUSB stack, frees prepared descriptors, and deletes the USB PHY when it was created by tinyusb_driver_install().</p>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Returns<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p><ul class="simple">
<li><p>ESP_OK on success</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE if the TinyUSB driver is not installed</p></li>
<li><p>Other error codes from TinyUSB task shutdown or USB PHY teardown </p></li>
</ul>
</p>
</dd>
</dl>
</dd></dl>

<dl class="cpp function">
<dt class="sig sig-object cpp" id="_CPPv421tinyusb_remote_wakeupv">
<span id="_CPPv321tinyusb_remote_wakeupv"></span><span id="_CPPv221tinyusb_remote_wakeupv"></span><span id="tinyusb_remote_wakeup__void"></span><span class="target" id="tinyusb_8h_1a7b63d4a37ad8be9974b8034fa196f975"></span><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.espressif.com/projects/esp-idf/en/latest/esp32s3/api-reference/system/esp_err.html#_CPPv49esp_err_t" title="(in ESP-IDF Programming Guide vmaster)"><span class="n"><span class="pre">esp_err_t</span></span></a><span class="w"> </span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="n"><span class="pre">tinyusb_remote_wakeup</span></span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><span class="kt"><span class="pre">void</span></span><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="headerlink" href="#_CPPv421tinyusb_remote_wakeupv" title="Permalink to this definition"></a><br /></dt>
<dd><p>Send a remote wakeup signal to the USB host. </p>
<div class="admonition note">
<p class="admonition-title">Note</p>
<p>Call this function only while the device is suspended and the host has enabled remote wakeup.</p>
</div>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Returns<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p><ul class="simple">
<li><p>ESP_OK on success</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE if the TinyUSB driver is not installed, or remote wakeup is not enabled by the host</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_NOT_ALLOWED if the USB device is not suspended</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_FAIL if the remote wakeup request cannot be sent</p></li>
<li><p>Other error codes from PM lock acquire when <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">CONFIG_TINYUSB_PM</span></code> is enabled </p></li>
</ul>
</p>
</dd>
</dl>
</dd></dl>

<dl class="cpp function">
<dt class="sig sig-object cpp" id="_CPPv426tinyusb_pm_get_lock_statusPb">
<span id="_CPPv326tinyusb_pm_get_lock_statusPb"></span><span id="_CPPv226tinyusb_pm_get_lock_statusPb"></span><span id="tinyusb_pm_get_lock_status__bP"></span><span class="target" id="tinyusb_8h_1a3ea8e66d5f669da0a8d897b90356609b"></span><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.espressif.com/projects/esp-idf/en/latest/esp32s3/api-reference/system/esp_err.html#_CPPv49esp_err_t" title="(in ESP-IDF Programming Guide vmaster)"><span class="n"><span class="pre">esp_err_t</span></span></a><span class="w"> </span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="n"><span class="pre">tinyusb_pm_get_lock_status</span></span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><span class="kt"><span class="pre">bool</span></span><span class="w"> </span><span class="p"><span class="pre">*</span></span><span class="n sig-param"><span class="pre">acquired</span></span><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="headerlink" href="#_CPPv426tinyusb_pm_get_lock_statusPb" title="Permalink to this definition"></a><br /></dt>
<dd><p>Get TinyUSB PM lock acquisition state. </p>
<p>Returns whether the <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">ESP_PM_NO_LIGHT_SLEEP</span></code> lock managed by esp_tinyusb is currently held.</p>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Parameters<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p><strong>acquired</strong> – <strong>[out]</strong> Whether the PM lock is currently held</p>
</dd>
<dt class="field-even">Returns<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-even"><p><ul class="simple">
<li><p>ESP_OK on success</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG if <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">acquired</span></code> is NULL</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE if the TinyUSB driver is not installed or the PM lock is not enabled </p></li>
</ul>
</p>
</dd>
</dl>
</dd></dl>

<section id="id3">
<h3>Macros<a class="headerlink" href="#id3" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>
<dl class="cpp macro">
<dt class="sig sig-object cpp" id="c.TINYUSB_DEFAULT_CONFIG">
<span class="target" id="tinyusb__default__config_8h_1a2e0268932d7ca04ff6fbebe466bc9117"></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="n"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_DEFAULT_CONFIG</span></span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><span class="p"><span class="pre">...</span></span><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="headerlink" href="#c.TINYUSB_DEFAULT_CONFIG" title="Permalink to this definition"></a><br /></dt>
<dd><p>Initialize a TinyUSB driver configuration with target defaults. </p>
<p>Supported invocations are:<ul class="simple">
<li><p><code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_DEFAULT_CONFIG()</span></code></p></li>
<li><p><code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_DEFAULT_CONFIG(event_cb)</span></code></p></li>
<li><p><code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_DEFAULT_CONFIG(event_cb,</span> <span class="pre">event_arg)</span></code></p></li>
</ul>
</p>
<p>The default port is <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_PORT_HIGH_SPEED_0</span></code> on ESP32-P4 and ESP32-S31. <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_PORT_FULL_SPEED_0</span></code> on other supported targets. The default task settings come from TINYUSB_TASK_DEFAULT(). </p>
</dd></dl>

<dl class="cpp macro">
<dt class="sig sig-object cpp" id="c.TINYUSB_DEFAULT_TASK_AFFINITY">
<span class="target" id="tinyusb__default__config_8h_1a9418497cd2166df883b5c502480975f8"></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="n"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_DEFAULT_TASK_AFFINITY</span></span></span><a class="headerlink" href="#c.TINYUSB_DEFAULT_TASK_AFFINITY" title="Permalink to this definition"></a><br /></dt>
<dd><p>Default TinyUSB task affinity. </p>
<p>The default task runs on CPU0 in unicore builds and CPU1 in multicore builds. </p>
</dd></dl>

<dl class="cpp macro">
<dt class="sig sig-object cpp" id="c.TINYUSB_DEFAULT_TASK_SIZE">
<span class="target" id="tinyusb__default__config_8h_1a1fe6e4a107b6d9f483dbe6fe4dbe67d1"></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="n"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_DEFAULT_TASK_SIZE</span></span></span><a class="headerlink" href="#c.TINYUSB_DEFAULT_TASK_SIZE" title="Permalink to this definition"></a><br /></dt>
<dd><p>Default TinyUSB task stack size in bytes. </p>
</dd></dl>

<dl class="cpp macro">
<dt class="sig sig-object cpp" id="c.TINYUSB_DEFAULT_TASK_PRIO">
<span class="target" id="tinyusb__default__config_8h_1a989a9eea5426174c1ec91d99ba4af38d"></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="n"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_DEFAULT_TASK_PRIO</span></span></span><a class="headerlink" href="#c.TINYUSB_DEFAULT_TASK_PRIO" title="Permalink to this definition"></a><br /></dt>

</dd></dl>

<dl class="cpp macro">
<dt class="sig sig-object cpp" id="c.TINYUSB_CONFIG_FULL_SPEED">
<span class="target" id="tinyusb__default__config_8h_1ab538306c56a772af369ca06154b10e7f"></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="n"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_CONFIG_FULL_SPEED</span></span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><span class="n"><span class="pre">event_hdl</span></span>, <span class="n"><span class="pre">arg</span></span><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="headerlink" href="#c.TINYUSB_CONFIG_FULL_SPEED" title="Permalink to this definition"></a><br /></dt>
<dd><p>Initialize a full-speed TinyUSB driver configuration. </p>
<p>The resulting initializer uses the default PHY settings, default task settings from TINYUSB_TASK_DEFAULT(), and empty descriptor pointers so the stack can fall back to built-in defaults when available.</p>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Parameters<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><ul class="simple">
<li><p><strong>event_hdl</strong> – Event callback assigned to <a class="reference internal" href="#structtinyusb__config__t_1af5bfbfa98d4b44e53b5a0f10839b3654"><span class="std std-ref">tinyusb_config_t.event_cb</span></a>. </p></li>
<li><p><strong>arg</strong> – User argument assigned to <a class="reference internal" href="#structtinyusb__config__t_1a36bdaa3ec01e54b771f5dd6257f11a7d"><span class="std std-ref">tinyusb_config_t.event_arg</span></a>. </p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</dd></dl>

<dl class="cpp macro">
<dt class="sig sig-object cpp" id="c.TINYUSB_TASK_DEFAULT">
<span class="target" id="tinyusb__default__config_8h_1a6e5dc5afa33c6f36808bd9f49ae096e6"></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="n"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_TASK_DEFAULT</span></span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="headerlink" href="#c.TINYUSB_TASK_DEFAULT" title="Permalink to this definition"></a><br /></dt>
<dd><p>Initialize a high-speed TinyUSB driver configuration. </p>
<p>The resulting initializer uses the default PHY settings, default task settings from TINYUSB_TASK_DEFAULT(), and empty descriptor pointers so the stack can fall back to built-in defaults when available.</p>
<p>
Initialize a TinyUSB task configuration with default values. </p>
<div class="admonition note">
<p class="admonition-title">Note</p>
<p>Only defined on high-speed-capable targets (e.g. ESP32-P4, ESP32-S31). Using this macro on a full-speed-only target results in a compile-time error; use TINYUSB_CONFIG_FULL_SPEED() there instead.</p>
</div>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Parameters<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><ul class="simple">
<li><p><strong>event_hdl</strong> – Event callback assigned to <a class="reference internal" href="#structtinyusb__config__t_1af5bfbfa98d4b44e53b5a0f10839b3654"><span class="std std-ref">tinyusb_config_t.event_cb</span></a>. </p></li>
<li><p><strong>arg</strong> – User argument assigned to <a class="reference internal" href="#structtinyusb__config__t_1a36bdaa3ec01e54b771f5dd6257f11a7d"><span class="std std-ref">tinyusb_config_t.event_arg</span></a>.</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</dd></dl>

<dl class="cpp macro">
<dt class="sig sig-object cpp" id="c.TINYUSB_TASK_CUSTOM">
<span class="target" id="tinyusb__default__config_8h_1a7dfe26683059270791fff4a6f71e460f"></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="n"><span class="pre">TINYUSB_TASK_CUSTOM</span></span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><span class="n"><span class="pre">s</span></span>, <span class="n"><span class="pre">p</span></span>, <span class="n"><span class="pre">a</span></span><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="headerlink" href="#c.TINYUSB_TASK_CUSTOM" title="Permalink to this definition"></a><br /></dt>

<dl class="cpp function">
<dt class="sig sig-object cpp" id="_CPPv426tinyusb_cdcacm_write_flush20tinyusb_cdcacm_itf_t8uint32_t">
<span id="_CPPv326tinyusb_cdcacm_write_flush20tinyusb_cdcacm_itf_t8uint32_t"></span><span id="_CPPv226tinyusb_cdcacm_write_flush20tinyusb_cdcacm_itf_t8uint32_t"></span><span id="tinyusb_cdcacm_write_flush__tinyusb_cdcacm_itf_t.uint32_t"></span><span class="target" id="tinyusb__cdc__acm_8h_1ae56eee472acd5c32ac333dc551fd1a84"></span><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.espressif.com/projects/esp-idf/en/latest/esp32s3/api-reference/system/esp_err.html#_CPPv49esp_err_t" title="(in ESP-IDF Programming Guide vmaster)"><span class="n"><span class="pre">esp_err_t</span></span></a><span class="w"> </span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="n"><span class="pre">tinyusb_cdcacm_write_flush</span></span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv420tinyusb_cdcacm_itf_t" title="tinyusb_cdcacm_itf_t"><span class="n"><span class="pre">tinyusb_cdcacm_itf_t</span></span></a><span class="w"> </span><span class="n sig-param"><span class="pre">itf</span></span>, <span class="n"><span class="pre">uint32_t</span></span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n sig-param"><span class="pre">timeout_ticks</span></span><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="headerlink" href="#_CPPv426tinyusb_cdcacm_write_flush20tinyusb_cdcacm_itf_t8uint32_t" title="Permalink to this definition"></a><br /></dt>
<dd><p>Flush queued data from a CDC ACM TX buffer. </p>
<p>Use tinyusb_cdcacm_write_queue() to add data to the buffer before calling this function.</p>
<div class="admonition note">
<p class="admonition-title">Note</p>
<p>Avoid calling this function with a timeout from CDC callbacks. TinyUSB may defer endpoint flushing until callback processing completes, which can cause the flush to block until the timeout expires.</p>
</div>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Parameters<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><ul class="simple">
<li><p><strong>itf</strong> – <strong>[in]</strong> CDC ACM interface number. </p></li>
<li><p><strong>timeout_ticks</strong> – <strong>[in]</strong> Flush timeout in RTOS ticks. Set to 0 for non-blocking mode.</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
<dt class="field-even">Returns<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-even"><p><ul class="simple">
<li><p>ESP_OK on success</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_NOT_FINISHED if data is still pending in non-blocking mode</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT if the timeout expires before the flush completes</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_FAIL if the interface is not initialized </p></li>
</ul>
</p>
</dd>
</dl>
</dd></dl>

<dl class="cpp function">
<dt class="sig sig-object cpp" id="_CPPv419tinyusb_cdcacm_read20tinyusb_cdcacm_itf_tP7uint8_t6size_tP6size_t">
<span id="_CPPv319tinyusb_cdcacm_read20tinyusb_cdcacm_itf_tP7uint8_t6size_tP6size_t"></span><span id="_CPPv219tinyusb_cdcacm_read20tinyusb_cdcacm_itf_tP7uint8_t6size_tP6size_t"></span><span id="tinyusb_cdcacm_read__tinyusb_cdcacm_itf_t.uint8_tP.s.sP"></span><span class="target" id="tinyusb__cdc__acm_8h_1a8be33d0f57d8df1777b2fbd5defd22ca"></span><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.espressif.com/projects/esp-idf/en/latest/esp32s3/api-reference/system/esp_err.html#_CPPv49esp_err_t" title="(in ESP-IDF Programming Guide vmaster)"><span class="n"><span class="pre">esp_err_t</span></span></a><span class="w"> </span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="n"><span class="pre">tinyusb_cdcacm_read</span></span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><a class="reference internal" href="#_CPPv420tinyusb_cdcacm_itf_t" title="tinyusb_cdcacm_itf_t"><span class="n"><span class="pre">tinyusb_cdcacm_itf_t</span></span></a><span class="w"> </span><span class="n sig-param"><span class="pre">itf</span></span>, <span class="n"><span class="pre">uint8_t</span></span><span class="w"> </span><span class="p"><span class="pre">*</span></span><span class="n sig-param"><span class="pre">out_buf</span></span>, <span class="n"><span class="pre">size_t</span></span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n sig-param"><span class="pre">out_buf_sz</span></span>, <span class="n"><span class="pre">size_t</span></span><span class="w"> </span><span class="p"><span class="pre">*</span></span><span class="n sig-param"><span class="pre">rx_data_size</span></span><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="headerlink" href="#_CPPv419tinyusb_cdcacm_read20tinyusb_cdcacm_itf_tP7uint8_t6size_tP6size_t" title="Permalink to this definition"></a><br /></dt>

<li><p><strong>out_buf_sz</strong> – <strong>[in]</strong> Output buffer size in bytes. </p></li>
<li><p><strong>rx_data_size</strong> – <strong>[out]</strong> Number of bytes written to <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">out_buf</span></code>. Must not be NULL.</p></li>

<dt class="field-even">Returns<span class="colon">:</span></dt>

</ul>
</p>
</dd>

<dt class="field-odd">Parameters<span class="colon">:</span></dt>

<li><p>ESP_ERR_NOT_SUPPORTED if the TinyUSB MSC buffer is smaller than the wear levelling sector size</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_NO_MEM if memory allocation fails</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_FAIL if the storage cannot be mapped to a LUN</p></li>

<div class="admonition note">

<p>This function erases all data on the storage media. </p>
</div>
<div class="admonition note">
<p class="admonition-title">Note</p>
<p>The storage must be mounted to TINYUSB_MSC_STORAGE_MOUNT_APP.</p>

<li><p>ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE if the MSC driver is not initialized</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG if <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">handle</span></code> is NULL or the storage is not mounted to the application</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_NOT_FOUND if the media is not in the expected unformatted state</p></li>
<li><p>Other error codes from disk I/O, VFS, or FAT filesystem operations </p></li>

<dd><p>Get the current mount point of a storage instance. </p>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Parameters<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><ul class="simple">
<li><p><strong>handle</strong> – <strong>[in]</strong> Storage handle returned by a storage creation function. </p></li>
<li><p><strong>mount_point</strong> – <strong>[out]</strong> Current mount point.</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
<dt class="field-even">Returns<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-even"><p><ul class="simple">
<li><p>ESP_OK on success</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG if <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">mount_point</span></code> is NULL</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE if the MSC driver or storage is not initialized </p></li>
</ul>
</p>
</dd>
</dl>
</dd></dl>

</section>
