<dt class="sig sig-object cpp" id="_CPPv421tinyusb_net_send_syncPv8uint16_tPv10TickType_t">
<span id="_CPPv321tinyusb_net_send_syncPv8uint16_tPv10TickType_t"></span><span id="_CPPv221tinyusb_net_send_syncPv8uint16_tPv10TickType_t"></span><span id="tinyusb_net_send_sync__voidP.uint16_t.voidP.TickType_t"></span><span class="target" id="tinyusb__net_8h_1afc22ae4266353af6985e999b43471515"></span><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.espressif.com/projects/esp-idf/en/latest/esp32s3/api-reference/system/esp_err.html#_CPPv49esp_err_t" title="(in ESP-IDF Programming Guide vmaster)"><span class="n"><span class="pre">esp_err_t</span></span></a><span class="w"> </span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="n"><span class="pre">tinyusb_net_send_sync</span></span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><span class="kt"><span class="pre">void</span></span><span class="w"> </span><span class="p"><span class="pre">*</span></span><span class="n sig-param"><span class="pre">buffer</span></span>, <span class="n"><span class="pre">uint16_t</span></span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n sig-param"><span class="pre">len</span></span>, <span class="kt"><span class="pre">void</span></span><span class="w"> </span><span class="p"><span class="pre">*</span></span><span class="n sig-param"><span class="pre">buff_free_arg</span></span>, <span class="n"><span class="pre">TickType_t</span></span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n sig-param"><span class="pre">timeout</span></span><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="headerlink" href="#_CPPv421tinyusb_net_send_syncPv8uint16_tPv10TickType_t" title="Permalink to this definition"></a><br /></dt>
<dd><p>Send a packet synchronously through the TinyUSB NET interface. </p>
<div class="admonition note">
<p class="admonition-title">Note</p>
<p>Synchronous and asynchronous sends can be mixed. </p>
</div>
<div class="admonition note">
<p class="admonition-title">Note</p>
<p>The first synchronous send allocates synchronization primitives, which increases heap usage.</p>
</div>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Parameters<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><ul class="simple">
<li><p><strong>buffer</strong> – <strong>[in]</strong> Packet payload buffer. </p></li>
<li><p><strong>len</strong> – <strong>[in]</strong> Packet length in bytes. </p></li>
<li><p><strong>buff_free_arg</strong> – <strong>[in]</strong> User token passed to <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">free_tx_buffer</span></code>, typically the packet buffer pointer. </p></li>
<li><p><strong>timeout</strong> – <strong>[in]</strong> Timeout in RTOS ticks.</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
<dt class="field-even">Returns<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-even"><p><ul class="simple">
<li><p>ESP_OK if the packet is accepted by TinyUSB for transmission</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_FAIL if the USB interface cannot accept the packet</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT if the transmission does not complete before <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">timeout</span></code></p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE if the TinyUSB NET interface is not mounted</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_NO_MEM if internal synchronization objects cannot be allocated </p></li>
</ul>
</p>
</dd>
</dl>
</dd></dl>

<dl class="cpp function">
<dt class="sig sig-object cpp" id="_CPPv422tinyusb_net_send_asyncPv8uint16_tPv">
<span id="_CPPv322tinyusb_net_send_asyncPv8uint16_tPv"></span><span id="_CPPv222tinyusb_net_send_asyncPv8uint16_tPv"></span><span id="tinyusb_net_send_async__voidP.uint16_t.voidP"></span><span class="target" id="tinyusb__net_8h_1ace895108c6332b99752a3e6cf0353ba9"></span><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.espressif.com/projects/esp-idf/en/latest/esp32s3/api-reference/system/esp_err.html#_CPPv49esp_err_t" title="(in ESP-IDF Programming Guide vmaster)"><span class="n"><span class="pre">esp_err_t</span></span></a><span class="w"> </span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="n"><span class="pre">tinyusb_net_send_async</span></span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><span class="kt"><span class="pre">void</span></span><span class="w"> </span><span class="p"><span class="pre">*</span></span><span class="n sig-param"><span class="pre">buffer</span></span>, <span class="n"><span class="pre">uint16_t</span></span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n sig-param"><span class="pre">len</span></span>, <span class="kt"><span class="pre">void</span></span><span class="w"> </span><span class="p"><span class="pre">*</span></span><span class="n sig-param"><span class="pre">buff_free_arg</span></span><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="headerlink" href="#_CPPv422tinyusb_net_send_asyncPv8uint16_tPv" title="Permalink to this definition"></a><br /></dt>
<dd><p>Queue a packet for asynchronous transmission through the TinyUSB NET interface. </p>
<div class="admonition note">
<p class="admonition-title">Note</p>
<p>When using asynchronous sends, free the packet through <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">free_tx_buffer</span></code> or another application-managed path. </p>
</div>
<div class="admonition note">
<p class="admonition-title">Note</p>
<p>Synchronous and asynchronous sends can be mixed. </p>
</div>
<div class="admonition note">
<p class="admonition-title">Note</p>
<p><code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">ESP_OK</span></code> means the packet was queued for processing in the TinyUSB task. It does not guarantee that the USB interface accepted the packet.</p>
</div>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Parameters<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><ul class="simple">
<li><p><strong>buffer</strong> – <strong>[in]</strong> Packet payload buffer. </p></li>
<li><p><strong>len</strong> – <strong>[in]</strong> Packet length in bytes. </p></li>
<li><p><strong>buff_free_arg</strong> – <strong>[in]</strong> User token passed to <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">free_tx_buffer</span></code>, typically the packet buffer pointer.</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
<dt class="field-even">Returns<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-even"><p><ul class="simple">
<li><p>ESP_OK if the packet is queued for deferred processing</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE if the TinyUSB NET interface is not mounted </p></li>
</ul>
</p>
</dd>
</dl>
</dd></dl>

<dd><p>Register a TinyUSB CDC interface in VFS. </p>
<p>Only one TinyUSB CDC interface can be registered in VFS at a time.</p>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Parameters<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><ul class="simple">
<li><p><strong>cdc_intf</strong> – <strong>[in]</strong> TinyUSB CDC interface number. </p></li>
<li><p><strong>path</strong> – <strong>[in]</strong> VFS path to register. Set to NULL to use VFS_TUSB_PATH_DEFAULT.</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
<dt class="field-even">Returns<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-even"><p><ul class="simple">
<li><p>ESP_OK on success</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE if the selected CDC interface is not initialized</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG if <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">path</span></code> is too long</p></li>
<li><p>Other error codes from VFS registration </p></li>
</ul>
</p>
</dd>
</dl>
</dd></dl>

<dt class="sig sig-object cpp" id="_CPPv436esp_vfs_tusb_cdc_set_tx_line_endings18esp_line_endings_t">
<span id="_CPPv336esp_vfs_tusb_cdc_set_tx_line_endings18esp_line_endings_t"></span><span id="_CPPv236esp_vfs_tusb_cdc_set_tx_line_endings18esp_line_endings_t"></span><span id="esp_vfs_tusb_cdc_set_tx_line_endings__esp_line_endings_t"></span><span class="target" id="vfs__tinyusb_8h_1a56cfad89983ffa60f3d1acbacf01d930"></span><span class="kt"><span class="pre">void</span></span><span class="w"> </span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="n"><span class="pre">esp_vfs_tusb_cdc_set_tx_line_endings</span></span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><span class="n"><span class="pre">esp_line_endings_t</span></span><span class="w"> </span><span class="n sig-param"><span class="pre">mode</span></span><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="headerlink" href="#_CPPv436esp_vfs_tusb_cdc_set_tx_line_endings18esp_line_endings_t" title="Permalink to this definition"></a><br /></dt>
<dd><p>Set the transmitted line ending conversion mode. </p>
<p>This controls how newline characters (<code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">\n</span></code>) written to stdout are converted before they are sent over the TinyUSB CDC VFS stream:</p>
<p><ul class="simple">
<li><p>ESP_LINE_ENDINGS_CRLF: convert LF to CRLF</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_LINE_ENDINGS_CR: convert LF to CR</p></li>
<li><p>ESP_LINE_ENDINGS_LF: no modification</p></li>
</ul>
</p>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Parameters<span class="colon">:</span></dt>

<dd><p>Set the received line ending conversion mode. </p>

<p><ul class="simple">
<li><p>ESP_LINE_ENDINGS_CRLF: convert CRLF to LF</p></li>

<li><p>ESP_LINE_ENDINGS_LF: no modification</p></li>
</ul>

<section id="kconfig-reference">
<h2>Kconfig Reference<a class="headerlink" href="#kconfig-reference" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>The generated Kconfig reference below documents the available <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">CONFIG_TINYUSB_*</span></code> options for esp_tinyusb, including descriptor defaults and class-specific settings.</p>
</section>
<section id="tinyusb-stack">
<span id="id23"></span><h2>TinyUSB Stack<a class="headerlink" href="#tinyusb-stack" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>Contains:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-bluetooth-host-class-bth"><span class="std std-ref">Bluetooth Host Class (BTH)</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-communication-device-class-cdc"><span class="std std-ref">Communication Device Class (CDC)</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-descriptor-configuration"><span class="std std-ref">Descriptor configuration</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-device-firmware-upgrade-dfu"><span class="std std-ref">Device Firmware Upgrade (DFU)</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-human-interface-device-class-hid"><span class="std std-ref">Human Interface Device Class (HID)</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-mass-storage-class-msc"><span class="std std-ref">Mass Storage Class (MSC)</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-media-transfer-protocol-mtp"><span class="std std-ref">Media Transfer Protocol (MTP)</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-musical-instrument-digital-interface-midi"><span class="std std-ref">Musical Instrument Digital Interface (MIDI)</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-network-driver-ecm-ncm-rndis"><span class="std std-ref">Network driver (ECM/NCM/RNDIS)</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-power-management"><span class="std std-ref">Power management</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-tinyusb-callbacks"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB callbacks</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-tinyusb-dcd"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB DCD</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-debug-level"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_DEBUG_LEVEL</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-vendor-specific-interface"><span class="std std-ref">Vendor Specific Interface</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
<section id="config-tinyusb-debug-level">
<span id="id24"></span><h3>CONFIG_TINYUSB_DEBUG_LEVEL<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-debug-level" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>

</dd>

<section id="tinyusb-dcd">
<span id="tinyusb-stack-tinyusb-dcd"></span><h3>TinyUSB DCD<a class="headerlink" href="#tinyusb-dcd" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>
<p>Contains:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-mode"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MODE</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
<section id="config-tinyusb-mode">
<span id="id25"></span><h4>CONFIG_TINYUSB_MODE<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-mode" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<blockquote>
<div><p>DCD Mode</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-tinyusb-dcd"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB DCD</span></a></p>
<p>TinyUSB DCD DWC2 Driver supports two modes: Slave mode (based on IRQ) and Buffer DMA mode.</p>
<p>Available options:</p>
<blockquote>
<div><blockquote>
<div></div></blockquote>
<ul class="simple" id="config-tinyusb-mode-slave">
<li><p>Slave/IRQ            (CONFIG_TINYUSB_MODE_SLAVE)</p>
</li>
<li id="config-tinyusb-mode-dma"><p>Buffer DMA           (CONFIG_TINYUSB_MODE_DMA)</p></li>
</ul>
</div></blockquote>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
</section>
<section id="tinyusb-callbacks">
<span id="tinyusb-stack-tinyusb-callbacks"></span><h3>TinyUSB callbacks<a class="headerlink" href="#tinyusb-callbacks" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>
<p>Contains:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-resume-callback"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_RESUME_CALLBACK</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-suspend-callback"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_SUSPEND_CALLBACK</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
<section id="config-tinyusb-suspend-callback">
<span id="id26"></span><h4>CONFIG_TINYUSB_SUSPEND_CALLBACK<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-suspend-callback" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<blockquote>
<div><p>Register suspend callback</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-tinyusb-callbacks"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB callbacks</span></a></p>
<p>Register TinyUSB’s suspend callback (tud_suspend_cb()) in esp_tinyusb.</p>
<p>When enabled, esp_tinyusb provides a strong implementation of
tud_suspend_cb() and dispatches TINYUSB_EVENT_SUSPENDED via the
esp_tinyusb device event callback.</p>
<p>When disabled, tinyusb provides weak implementation of the tud_suspend_cb(),
and user can provide it’s own strong implementation of the tud_suspend_cb().</p>
<p>NOTE: When this option is enabled, user applications MUST NOT
define tud_suspend_cb() themselves. Defining tud_suspend_cb()
in the application while this option is enabled will result in
a linker error due to multiple definitions.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>No (disabled)</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
<section id="config-tinyusb-resume-callback">
<span id="id27"></span><h4>CONFIG_TINYUSB_RESUME_CALLBACK<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-resume-callback" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<blockquote>
<div><p>Register resume callback</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-tinyusb-callbacks"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB callbacks</span></a></p>
<p>Register TinyUSB’s resume callback (tud_resume_cb()) in esp_tinyusb.</p>
<p>When enabled, esp_tinyusb provides a strong implementation of
tud_resume_cb() and dispatches TINYUSB_EVENT_RESUMED via the
esp_tinyusb device event callback.</p>
<p>When disabled, tinyusb provides weak implementation of the tud_resume_cb(),
and user can provide it’s own strong implementation of the tud_resume_cb().</p>
<p>NOTE: When this option is enabled, user applications MUST NOT
define tud_resume_cb() themselves. Defining tud_resume_cb()
in the application while this option is enabled will result in
a linker error due to multiple definitions.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>No (disabled)</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
</section>
<section id="tinyusb-stack-power-management">
<span id="id28"></span><h3>Power management<a class="headerlink" href="#tinyusb-stack-power-management" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>
<p>Contains:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-pm"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_PM</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-usb-otg-wakeup"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_USB_OTG_WAKEUP</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
<section id="config-tinyusb-usb-otg-wakeup">
<span id="id29"></span><h4>CONFIG_TINYUSB_USB_OTG_WAKEUP<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-usb-otg-wakeup" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<blockquote>
<div><p>USB Device light sleep wakeup source</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-power-management"><span class="std std-ref">Power management</span></a></p>
<p>Register light sleep event callbacks to prepare the UTMI PHY for USB wakeup when
entering light sleep while the USB bus is suspended.</p>
<p>Available only on USB HS ports, due to PHY limitation. The USB wakeup source
(esp_sleep_enable_usb_wakeup()) is enabled automatically during
tinyusb_driver_install(). The USB connection power domain must be kept on during
light sleep.</p>
<p>Requires the TinyUSB suspend and resume callbacks (TINYUSB_SUSPEND_CALLBACK and
TINYUSB_RESUME_CALLBACK) to be registered inside esp_tinyusb, so the driver is
notified about USB suspend/resume. Without them the wakeup state cannot be tracked
and the feature is not usable.</p>
<p>Independent of TinyUSB Power Management (PM locks). USB wakeup works whether light
sleep is entered manually or triggered automatically after the PM lock is released
on USB suspend.</p>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
<section id="config-tinyusb-pm">
<span id="id30"></span><h4>CONFIG_TINYUSB_PM<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-pm" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<blockquote>
<div><p>Enable tinyusb Power Management</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-power-management"><span class="std std-ref">Power management</span></a></p>
<p>Enable TinyUSB power management using ESP-IDF PM locks.</p>
<p>When enabled together with <cite>tinyusb_config_t.pm_lock_enable</cite>, esp_tinyusb creates an
ESP_PM_NO_LIGHT_SLEEP lock and releases it while the USB device is suspended, allowing
automatic light sleep when automatic light sleep is configured through esp_pm_configure().</p>
<p>Requires the TinyUSB suspend and resume callbacks (TINYUSB_SUSPEND_CALLBACK and
TINYUSB_RESUME_CALLBACK) to be registered inside esp_tinyusb. The PM lock is released
on USB suspend and re-acquired on USB resume, so without those events the lock would
never be released and automatic light sleep would never happen.</p>
<p>It is recommended to pair this config with the TINYUSB_USB_OTG_WAKEUP config above, so the USB
peripheral can act as a wakeup source from the light sleep. Without enabling the TINYUSB_USB_OTG_WAKEUP
the USB peripheral can NOT act as a light sleep wakeup source.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>No (disabled) if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-suspend-callback"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_SUSPEND_CALLBACK</span></a> &amp;&amp; <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-resume-callback"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_RESUME_CALLBACK</span></a> &amp;&amp; PM_ENABLE &amp;&amp; FREERTOS_USE_TICKLESS_IDLE</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
</section>

<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-desc-product-string"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_DESC_PRODUCT_STRING</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-desc-serial-string"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_DESC_SERIAL_STRING</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-desc-custom-vid"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_DESC_CUSTOM_VID</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-desc-use-espressif-vid"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_DESC_USE_ESPRESSIF_VID</span></a></p></li>

<section id="config-tinyusb-desc-use-espressif-vid">
<span id="id32"></span><h4>CONFIG_TINYUSB_DESC_USE_ESPRESSIF_VID<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-desc-use-espressif-vid" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>

<blockquote>
<div><p>MSC FIFO size</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-mass-storage-class-msc"><span class="std std-ref">Mass Storage Class (MSC)</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-msc-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MSC_ENABLED</span></a></p>

</ul>
</dd>
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>512 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-msc-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MSC_ENABLED</span></a></p></li>

</dd>
</dl>

</section>
<section id="config-tinyusb-msc-mount-path">
<span id="id45"></span><h5>CONFIG_TINYUSB_MSC_MOUNT_PATH<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-msc-mount-path" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h5>
<blockquote>
<div><p>Mount Path</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-mass-storage-class-msc"><span class="std std-ref">Mass Storage Class (MSC)</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-msc-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MSC_ENABLED</span></a></p>
<p>MSC Mount Path of storage.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>“/data” if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-msc-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MSC_ENABLED</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
</section>
</section>
<section id="media-transfer-protocol-mtp">
<span id="tinyusb-stack-media-transfer-protocol-mtp"></span><h3>Media Transfer Protocol (MTP)<a class="headerlink" href="#media-transfer-protocol-mtp" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>
<p>Contains:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-mtp-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MTP_ENABLED</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
<section id="config-tinyusb-mtp-enabled">
<span id="id46"></span><h4>CONFIG_TINYUSB_MTP_ENABLED<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-mtp-enabled" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<blockquote>
<div><p>Enable TinyUSB MTP feature</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-media-transfer-protocol-mtp"><span class="std std-ref">Media Transfer Protocol (MTP)</span></a></p>
<p>Enable TinyUSB MTP feature. MTP exposes files through already-mounted
VFS/FATFS paths and does not take block-device ownership.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>No (disabled)</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
<section id="config-tinyusb-mtp-ep-bufsize">
<span id="id47"></span><h5>CONFIG_TINYUSB_MTP_EP_BUFSIZE<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-mtp-ep-bufsize" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h5>
<blockquote>
<div><p>MTP endpoint buffer size</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-media-transfer-protocol-mtp"><span class="std std-ref">Media Transfer Protocol (MTP)</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-mtp-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MTP_ENABLED</span></a></p>
<p>MTP bulk endpoint transfer buffer size, in bytes. Larger values improve
large file transfer throughput at the cost of RAM. The minimum size keeps
valid ObjectInfo and object property string datasets in one transfer buffer.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Range:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>from 4096 to 8192 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-mtp-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MTP_ENABLED</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>4096 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-mtp-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MTP_ENABLED</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
<section id="config-tinyusb-mtp-max-storages">
<span id="id48"></span><h5>CONFIG_TINYUSB_MTP_MAX_STORAGES<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-mtp-max-storages" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h5>
<blockquote>
<div><p>Maximum MTP storage count</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-media-transfer-protocol-mtp"><span class="std std-ref">Media Transfer Protocol (MTP)</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-mtp-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MTP_ENABLED</span></a></p>
<p>Maximum number of mounted VFS/FATFS paths that can be registered as MTP storages.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Range:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>from 1 to 3 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-mtp-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MTP_ENABLED</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>3 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-mtp-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MTP_ENABLED</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
<section id="config-tinyusb-mtp-max-objects">
<span id="id49"></span><h5>CONFIG_TINYUSB_MTP_MAX_OBJECTS<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-mtp-max-objects" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h5>
<blockquote>
<div><p>Maximum cached MTP object count</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-media-transfer-protocol-mtp"><span class="std std-ref">Media Transfer Protocol (MTP)</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-mtp-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MTP_ENABLED</span></a></p>
<p>Maximum number of file or directory object handles cached by the MTP backend.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Range:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>from 32 to 1024 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-mtp-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MTP_ENABLED</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>512 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-mtp-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MTP_ENABLED</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
<section id="config-tinyusb-mtp-trace-writes">
<span id="id50"></span><h5>CONFIG_TINYUSB_MTP_TRACE_WRITES<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-mtp-trace-writes" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h5>
<blockquote>
<div><p>Trace MTP file-transfer operations</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-media-transfer-protocol-mtp"><span class="std std-ref">Media Transfer Protocol (MTP)</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-mtp-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MTP_ENABLED</span></a></p>
<p>Enable concise logs for host read, write, edit, delete, rename, and unsupported
MTP operations. This is intended for manual host compatibility debugging.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>No (disabled) if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-mtp-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_MTP_ENABLED</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
</section>
</section>

<section id="config-tinyusb-cdc-count">
<span id="id52"></span><h5>CONFIG_TINYUSB_CDC_COUNT<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-cdc-count" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h5>
<blockquote>
<div><p>CDC Channel Count</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-communication-device-class-cdc"><span class="std std-ref">Communication Device Class (CDC)</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-cdc-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_CDC_ENABLED</span></a></p>

<dl class="simple">

</dd>
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">

</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
<section id="config-tinyusb-cdc-rx-bufsize">
<span id="id53"></span><h5>CONFIG_TINYUSB_CDC_RX_BUFSIZE<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-cdc-rx-bufsize" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h5>
<blockquote>
<div><p>CDC FIFO size of RX channel</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-communication-device-class-cdc"><span class="std std-ref">Communication Device Class (CDC)</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-cdc-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_CDC_ENABLED</span></a></p>
<p>This buffer size defines maximum data length in bytes that you can receive at once.
Must be greater or equal to TINYUSB_CDC_EP_BUFSIZE for correct receiving.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Range:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>from 64 to 10000 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-cdc-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_CDC_ENABLED</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>512 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-cdc-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_CDC_ENABLED</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
<section id="config-tinyusb-cdc-tx-bufsize">
<span id="id54"></span><h5>CONFIG_TINYUSB_CDC_TX_BUFSIZE<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-cdc-tx-bufsize" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h5>
<blockquote>
<div><p>CDC FIFO size of TX channel</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-communication-device-class-cdc"><span class="std std-ref">Communication Device Class (CDC)</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-cdc-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_CDC_ENABLED</span></a></p>
<p>This buffer size defines maximum data length in bytes that you can transmit at once.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>512 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-cdc-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_CDC_ENABLED</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
<section id="config-tinyusb-cdc-ep-bufsize">
<span id="id55"></span><h5>CONFIG_TINYUSB_CDC_EP_BUFSIZE<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-cdc-ep-bufsize" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h5>
<blockquote>
<div><p>CDC Endpoint buffer size</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-communication-device-class-cdc"><span class="std std-ref">Communication Device Class (CDC)</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-cdc-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_CDC_ENABLED</span></a></p>
<p>This low layer buffer has the most significant impact on performance. Set to 8192 for best performance.
Sizes above 8192 bytes bring only little performance improvement.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>512 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-cdc-enabled"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_CDC_ENABLED</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
</section>
</section>

<section id="network-driver-ecm-ncm-rndis">
<span id="tinyusb-stack-network-driver-ecm-ncm-rndis"></span><h3>Network driver (ECM/NCM/RNDIS)<a class="headerlink" href="#network-driver-ecm-ncm-rndis" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>

<ul class="simple">

<section id="config-tinyusb-net-mode">
<span id="id62"></span><h4>CONFIG_TINYUSB_NET_MODE<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-net-mode" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<blockquote>
<div><p>Network mode</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-network-driver-ecm-ncm-rndis"><span class="std std-ref">Network driver (ECM/NCM/RNDIS)</span></a></p>
<p>Select network driver you want to use.</p>
<p>Available options:</p>
<blockquote>
<div><blockquote>
<div></div></blockquote>
<ul class="simple" id="config-tinyusb-net-mode-ecm-rndis">
<li><p>ECM/RNDIS            (CONFIG_TINYUSB_NET_MODE_ECM_RNDIS)</p>
</li>
<li id="config-tinyusb-net-mode-ncm"><p>NCM                  (CONFIG_TINYUSB_NET_MODE_NCM)</p>
</li>
<li id="config-tinyusb-net-mode-none"><p>None                 (CONFIG_TINYUSB_NET_MODE_NONE)</p></li>
</ul>
</div></blockquote>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
<section id="config-tinyusb-ncm-out-ntb-buffs-count">
<span id="id63"></span><h4>CONFIG_TINYUSB_NCM_OUT_NTB_BUFFS_COUNT<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-ncm-out-ntb-buffs-count" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<blockquote>
<div><p>Number of NCM NTB buffers for reception side</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-network-driver-ecm-ncm-rndis"><span class="std std-ref">Network driver (ECM/NCM/RNDIS)</span></a></p>
<p>Number of NTB buffers for reception side.
Can be increased to improve performance and stability with the cost of additional RAM requirements.
Helps to mitigate “tud_network_can_xmit: request blocked” warning message when running NCM device.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Range:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>from 1 to 6 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-net-mode-ncm"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_NET_MODE_NCM</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>3 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-net-mode-ncm"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_NET_MODE_NCM</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
<section id="config-tinyusb-ncm-in-ntb-buffs-count">
<span id="id64"></span><h4>CONFIG_TINYUSB_NCM_IN_NTB_BUFFS_COUNT<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-ncm-in-ntb-buffs-count" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<blockquote>
<div><p>Number of NCM NTB buffers for transmission side</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-network-driver-ecm-ncm-rndis"><span class="std std-ref">Network driver (ECM/NCM/RNDIS)</span></a></p>
<p>Number of NTB buffers for transmission side.
Can be increased to improve performance and stability with the cost of additional RAM requirements.
Helps to mitigate “tud_network_can_xmit: request blocked” warning message when running NCM device.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Range:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>from 1 to 6 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-net-mode-ncm"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_NET_MODE_NCM</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>3 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-net-mode-ncm"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_NET_MODE_NCM</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
<section id="config-tinyusb-ncm-out-ntb-buff-max-size">
<span id="id65"></span><h4>CONFIG_TINYUSB_NCM_OUT_NTB_BUFF_MAX_SIZE<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-ncm-out-ntb-buff-max-size" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<blockquote>
<div><p>NCM NTB Buffer size for reception size</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-network-driver-ecm-ncm-rndis"><span class="std std-ref">Network driver (ECM/NCM/RNDIS)</span></a></p>
<p>Size of NTB buffers on the reception side. The minimum size used by Linux is 2048 bytes.
NTB buffer size must be significantly larger than the MTU (Maximum Transmission Unit).
The typical default MTU size for Ethernet is 1500 bytes, plus an additional packet overhead.
To improve performance, the NTB buffer size should be large enough to fit multiple MTU-sized
frames in a single NTB buffer and it’s length should be multiple of 4.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Range:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>from 1600 to 10240 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-net-mode-ncm"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_NET_MODE_NCM</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>3200 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-net-mode-ncm"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_NET_MODE_NCM</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
<section id="config-tinyusb-ncm-in-ntb-buff-max-size">
<span id="id66"></span><h4>CONFIG_TINYUSB_NCM_IN_NTB_BUFF_MAX_SIZE<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-ncm-in-ntb-buff-max-size" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<blockquote>
<div><p>NCM NTB Buffer size for transmission size</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-network-driver-ecm-ncm-rndis"><span class="std std-ref">Network driver (ECM/NCM/RNDIS)</span></a></p>
<p>Size of NTB buffers on the transmission side. The minimum size used by Linux is 2048 bytes.
NTB buffer size must be significantly larger than the MTU (Maximum Transmission Unit).
The typical default MTU size for Ethernet is 1500 bytes, plus an additional packet overhead.
To improve performance, the NTB buffer size should be large enough to fit multiple MTU-sized
frames in a single NTB buffer and it’s length should be multiple of 4.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Range:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>from 1600 to 10240 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-net-mode-ncm"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_NET_MODE_NCM</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>3200 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-net-mode-ncm"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_NET_MODE_NCM</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
</section>
<section id="vendor-specific-interface">
<span id="tinyusb-stack-vendor-specific-interface"></span><h3>Vendor Specific Interface<a class="headerlink" href="#vendor-specific-interface" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h3>
<p>Contains:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-vendor-count"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_VENDOR_COUNT</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-vendor-epsize"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_VENDOR_EPSIZE</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-vendor-rx-bufsize"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_VENDOR_RX_BUFSIZE</span></a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-vendor-tx-bufsize"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_VENDOR_TX_BUFSIZE</span></a></p></li>
</ul>
<section id="config-tinyusb-vendor-count">
<span id="id67"></span><h4>CONFIG_TINYUSB_VENDOR_COUNT<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-vendor-count" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<blockquote>
<div><p>TinyUSB Vendor specific interfaces count</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-vendor-specific-interface"><span class="std std-ref">Vendor Specific Interface</span></a></p>
<p>Setting value greater than 0 will enable TinyUSB Vendor specific feature.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Range:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>from 0 to 2</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>0</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
<section id="config-tinyusb-vendor-rx-bufsize">
<span id="id68"></span><h4>CONFIG_TINYUSB_VENDOR_RX_BUFSIZE<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-vendor-rx-bufsize" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<blockquote>
<div><p>Vendor specific FIFO size of RX channel</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-vendor-specific-interface"><span class="std std-ref">Vendor Specific Interface</span></a></p>
<p>This buffer size defines maximum data length in bytes that you can receive at once.</p>
<p>Note: Unlike other USB classes, the vendor class supports setting buffer sizes to 0
to disable internal buffering. When disabled, RX data goes directly to tud_vendor_rx_cb(),
the tud_vendor_read() function is not available and application must handle data in the callback.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Range:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>from 0 to 32768 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-vendor-count"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_VENDOR_COUNT</span></a> &gt; 0</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>64 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-vendor-count"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_VENDOR_COUNT</span></a> &gt; 0</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
<section id="config-tinyusb-vendor-tx-bufsize">
<span id="id69"></span><h4>CONFIG_TINYUSB_VENDOR_TX_BUFSIZE<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-vendor-tx-bufsize" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<blockquote>
<div><p>Vendor specific FIFO size of TX channel</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-vendor-specific-interface"><span class="std std-ref">Vendor Specific Interface</span></a></p>
<p>This buffer size defines maximum data length in bytes that you can transmit at once.</p>
<p>Note: Unlike other USB classes, the vendor class supports setting buffer sizes to 0
to disable internal buffering. When disabled, tud_vendor_write() bypasses internal software FIFO.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Range:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>from 0 to 32768 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-vendor-count"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_VENDOR_COUNT</span></a> &gt; 0</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>64 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-vendor-count"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_VENDOR_COUNT</span></a> &gt; 0</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
<section id="config-tinyusb-vendor-epsize">
<span id="id70"></span><h4>CONFIG_TINYUSB_VENDOR_EPSIZE<a class="headerlink" href="#config-tinyusb-vendor-epsize" title="Permalink to this heading"></a></h4>
<blockquote>
<div><p>Vendor specific Endpoint buffer size</p>
<p><em>Found in:</em> <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack"><span class="std std-ref">TinyUSB Stack</span></a> &gt; <a class="reference internal" href="#tinyusb-stack-vendor-specific-interface"><span class="std std-ref">Vendor Specific Interface</span></a></p>
<p>This low layer buffer has the most significant impact on performance. Set to 8192 for best performance.
Sizes above 8192 bytes bring only little performance improvement.</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Range:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>from 64 to 32768 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-vendor-count"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_VENDOR_COUNT</span></a> &gt; 0</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
<dt>Default value:</dt><dd><ul class="simple">
<li><p>64 if <a class="reference internal" href="#config-tinyusb-vendor-count"><span class="std std-ref">CONFIG_TINYUSB_VENDOR_COUNT</span></a> &gt; 0</p></li>
</ul>
</dd>
</dl>
</div></blockquote>
</section>
</section>
</section>
